                    <ul class="flex flex-col gap-2.5"><li><a href="#help" class="text-[#4A4A5A] text-[0.85rem] transition-colors hover:text-[#1A1A2E]">Help center</a></li><li><a href="#blog" class="text-[#4A4A5A] text-[0.85rem] transition-colors hover:text-[#1A1A2E]">Blog</a></li><li><a href="#communities" class="text-[#4A4A5A] text-[0.85rem] transition-colors hover:text-[#1A1A2E]">Community</a></li><li><a href="#" class="text-[#4A4A5A] text-[0.85rem] transition-colors hover:text-[#1A1A2E]">Trust &amp; safety</a></li></ul>

    <!-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════
    SCRIPTS
    ═══════════════════════════════════════════ -->
    <script>
        (function() {
            'use strict';

            // ── DOM Refs ──
            const nav = document.getElementById('mainNav');
            const navToggle = document.getElementById('navToggle');
            const mobileNav = document.getElementById('mobileNav');
            const mobileOverlay = document.getElementById('mobileOverlay');
            const mobileClose = document.getElementById('mobileClose');
            const body = document.body;
            const backToTop = document.getElementById('backToTop');
            const chatToggle = document.getElementById('chatToggle');
            const chatBubble = document.getElementById('chatBubble');

            // ── Nav scroll ──
            window.addEventListener('scroll', function() {
                nav.classList.toggle('scrolled', window.scrollY > 20);
                backToTop.classList.toggle('visible', window.scrollY > 400);
            }, { passive: true });

            // ── Back to top ──
            backToTop.addEventListener('click', function() {
                window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
            });

            // ── Mobile nav ──
            function openMobile() {
                mobileNav.classList.add('open');
                mobileOverlay.classList.add('open');
                navToggle.classList.add('active');
                navToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
                body.classList.add('overflow-hidden');
            }

            function closeMobile() {
                mobileNav.classList.remove('open');
                mobileOverlay.classList.remove('open');
                navToggle.classList.remove('active');
                navToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                body.classList.remove('overflow-hidden');
            }

            navToggle.addEventListener('click', function() {
                mobileNav.classList.contains('open') ? closeMobile() : openMobile();
            });

            mobileClose.addEventListener('click', closeMobile);
            mobileOverlay.addEventListener('click', closeMobile);

            document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
                if (e.key === 'Escape' && mobileNav.classList.contains('open')) closeMobile();
            });

            document.querySelectorAll('.mobile-nav a').forEach(function(link) {
                link.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                    closeMobile();
                    const targetId = this.getAttribute('href');
                    if (targetId && targetId.startsWith('#')) {
                        e.preventDefault();
                        const target = document.querySelector(targetId);
                        if (target) {
                            const offset = 90;
                            const top = target.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.pageYOffset - offset;
                            window.scrollTo({ top: top, behavior: 'smooth' });
                        }
                    }
                });
            });

            // ── Scroll reveal ──
            const revealObserver = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
                entries.forEach(function(entry) {
                    if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                        entry.target.classList.add('in');
                        revealObserver.unobserve(entry.target);
                    }
                });
            }, { threshold: 0.08, rootMargin: '0px 0px -40px 0px' });

            document.querySelectorAll('.reveal, .reveal-stagger').forEach(function(el) {
                revealObserver.observe(el);
            });

            // ── Counters ──
            const counters = document.querySelectorAll('[data-count]');
            const counterObserver = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
                entries.forEach(function(entry) {
                    if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
                    counterObserver.unobserve(entry.target);
                    const target = parseInt(entry.target.dataset.count, 10);
                    const duration = 1600;
                    const start = performance.now();

                    function updateCounter(now) {
                        const progress = Math.min((now - start) / duration, 1);
                        const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - progress, 3);
                        const value = target * eased;
                        entry.target.textContent = Math.round(value).toLocaleString('en-US');
                        if (progress < 1) requestAnimationFrame(updateCounter);
                    }
                    requestAnimationFrame(updateCounter);
                });
            }, { threshold: 0.3 });

            counters.forEach(function(el) { counterObserver.observe(el); });

            // ── Live counter ──
            const onlineCountEl = document.getElementById('onlineCount');
            setInterval(function() {
                const cur = parseInt(onlineCountEl.textContent.replace(/,/g, ''), 10);
                const next = Math.max(2800, cur + Math.round((Math.random() - 0.5) * 16));
                onlineCountEl.textContent = next.toLocaleString('en-US');
            }, 4500);

            // ── AI Chat ──
            let chatOpen = false;
            chatToggle.addEventListener('click', function() {
                chatOpen = !chatOpen;
                chatToggle.classList.toggle('active', chatOpen);
                chatBubble.classList.toggle('open', chatOpen);
                chatToggle.querySelector('.icon').innerHTML = chatOpen ? '<i class="bi bi-x-lg"></i>' :
                    '<i class="bi bi-chat-dots-fill"></i>';
            });

            // ── Chat options ──
            document.querySelectorAll('.chat-option').forEach(function(btn) {
                btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                    const response = this.dataset.response;
                    const msgEl = document.getElementById('chatMessage');
                    msgEl.textContent = '';
                    let i = 0;
                    const chars = response.split('');
                    const interval = setInterval(function() {
                        if (i < chars.length) {
                            msgEl.textContent += chars[i];
                            i++;
                        } else {
                            clearInterval(interval);
                        }
                    }, 12);
                });
            });

            // ── Smooth anchor scroll ──
            document.querySelectorAll('a[href^="#"]').forEach(function(anchor) {
                anchor.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                    const targetId = this.getAttribute('href');
                    if (targetId === '#') return;
                    const target = document.querySelector(targetId);
                    if (target) {
                        e.preventDefault();
                        const offset = 90;
                        const top = target.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.pageYOffset - offset;
                        window.scrollTo({ top: top, behavior: 'smooth' });
                        if (mobileNav.classList.contains('open')) closeMobile();
                    }
                });
            });

            // ── Ripple ──
            document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                const btn = e.target.closest('.btn-gold, .btn-outline, .btn-jade, .btn-view-more');
                if (!btn) return;
                const rect = btn.getBoundingClientRect();
                const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
                const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
                const ripple = document.createElement('span');
                ripple.className = 'ripple';
                ripple.style.cssText =
                    'position:absolute;border-radius:50%;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.25);width:20px;height:20px;left:' +
                    (x - 10) + 'px;top:' + (y - 10) + 'px;pointer-events:none;transform:scale(0);animation:rippleAnim 0.6s ease-out forwards;';
                btn.style.position = 'relative';
                btn.style.overflow = 'hidden';
                btn.appendChild(ripple);
                setTimeout(function() { ripple.remove(); }, 650);
            });

            if (!document.getElementById('rippleStyles')) {
                const style = document.createElement('style');
                style.id = 'rippleStyles';
                style.textContent = '@keyframes rippleAnim { to { transform: scale(4); opacity: 0; } }';
                document.head.appendChild(style);
            }

            console.log('✦ KnotWork — Light & Airy Edition ✦');
            console.log('✦ Explore · Mentors · Communities · Pricing · About · Blog · Help ✦');

        })();
    </script>

</body>
</html>

    <!-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════
    SCRIPTS
    ═══════════════════════════════════════════ -->
    <script>
        (function() {
            'use strict';

            // ── DOM Refs ──
            const nav = document.getElementById('mainNav');
            const navToggle = document.getElementById('navToggle');
            const mobileNav = document.getElementById('mobileNav');
            const mobileOverlay = document.getElementById('mobileOverlay');
            const mobileClose = document.getElementById('mobileClose');
            const body = document.body;
            const backToTop = document.getElementById('backToTop');
            const chatToggle = document.getElementById('chatToggle');
            const chatBubble = document.getElementById('chatBubble');

            // ── Nav scroll ──
            window.addEventListener('scroll', function() {
                nav.classList.toggle('scrolled', window.scrollY > 20);
                backToTop.classList.toggle('visible', window.scrollY > 400);
            }, { passive: true });

            // ── Back to top ──
            backToTop.addEventListener('click', function() {
                window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
            });

            // ── Mobile nav ──
            function openMobile() {
                mobileNav.classList.add('open');
                mobileOverlay.classList.add('open');
                navToggle.classList.add('active');
                navToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
                body.classList.add('overflow-hidden');
            }

            function closeMobile() {
                mobileNav.classList.remove('open');
                mobileOverlay.classList.remove('open');
                navToggle.classList.remove('active');
                navToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                body.classList.remove('overflow-hidden');
            }

            navToggle.addEventListener('click', function() {
                mobileNav.classList.contains('open') ? closeMobile() : openMobile();
            });

            mobileClose.addEventListener('click', closeMobile);
            mobileOverlay.addEventListener('click', closeMobile);

            document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
                if (e.key === 'Escape' && mobileNav.classList.contains('open')) closeMobile();
            });

            // ── Scroll reveal ──
            const revealObserver = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
                entries.forEach(function(entry) {
                    if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                        entry.target.classList.add('in');
                        revealObserver.unobserve(entry.target);
                    }
                });
            }, { threshold: 0.08, rootMargin: '0px 0px -40px 0px' });

            document.querySelectorAll('.reveal, .reveal-stagger').forEach(function(el) {
                revealObserver.observe(el);
            });

            // ── AI Chat ──
            let chatOpen = false;
            chatToggle.addEventListener('click', function() {
                chatOpen = !chatOpen;
                chatToggle.classList.toggle('active', chatOpen);
                chatBubble.classList.toggle('open', chatOpen);
                chatToggle.querySelector('.icon').innerHTML = chatOpen ? '<i class="bi bi-x-lg"></i>' :
                    '<i class="bi bi-chat-dots-fill"></i>';
            });

            // ── Chat options ──
            document.querySelectorAll('.chat-option').forEach(function(btn) {
                btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                    const response = this.dataset.response;
                    const msgEl = document.getElementById('chatMessage');
                    msgEl.textContent = '';
                    let i = 0;
                    const chars = response.split('');
                    const interval = setInterval(function() {
                        if (i < chars.length) {
                            msgEl.textContent += chars[i];
                            i++;
                        } else {
                            clearInterval(interval);
                        }
                    }, 12);
                });
            });

            // ── Filter chips ──
            document.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip').forEach(function(chip) {
                chip.addEventListener('click', function() {
                    document.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                    this.classList.add('active');
                });
            });

            // ── Ripple ──
            document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                const btn = e.target.closest('.btn-gold, .btn-outline, .btn-view-more');
                if (!btn) return;
                const rect = btn.getBoundingClientRect();
                const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
                const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
                const ripple = document.createElement('span');
                ripple.className = 'ripple';
                ripple.style.cssText =
                    'position:absolute;border-radius:50%;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.25);width:20px;height:20px;left:' +
                    (x - 10) + 'px;top:' + (y - 10) + 'px;pointer-events:none;transform:scale(0);animation:rippleAnim 0.6s ease-out forwards;';
                btn.style.position = 'relative';
                btn.style.overflow = 'hidden';
                btn.appendChild(ripple);
                setTimeout(function() { ripple.remove(); }, 650);
            });

            if (!document.getElementById('rippleStyles')) {
                const style = document.createElement('style');
                style.id = 'rippleStyles';
                style.textContent = '@keyframes rippleAnim { to { transform: scale(4); opacity: 0; } }';
                document.head.appendChild(style);
            }

            console.log('✦ KnotWork — Explore ✦');

        })();
    </script>

</body>
</html>

    <!-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════
    SCRIPTS
    ═══════════════════════════════════════════ -->
    <script>
        (function() {
            'use strict';

            const nav = document.getElementById('mainNav');
            const navToggle = document.getElementById('navToggle');
            const mobileNav = document.getElementById('mobileNav');
            const mobileOverlay = document.getElementById('mobileOverlay');
            const mobileClose = document.getElementById('mobileClose');
            const body = document.body;
            const backToTop = document.getElementById('backToTop');
            const chatToggle = document.getElementById('chatToggle');
            const chatBubble = document.getElementById('chatBubble');

            window.addEventListener('scroll', function() {
                nav.classList.toggle('scrolled', window.scrollY > 20);
                backToTop.classList.toggle('visible', window.scrollY > 400);
            }, { passive: true });

            backToTop.addEventListener('click', function() {
                window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
            });

            function openMobile() {
                mobileNav.classList.add('open');
                mobileOverlay.classList.add('open');
                navToggle.classList.add('active');
                navToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
                body.classList.add('overflow-hidden');
            }

            function closeMobile() {
                mobileNav.classList.remove('open');
                mobileOverlay.classList.remove('open');
                navToggle.classList.remove('active');
                navToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                body.classList.remove('overflow-hidden');
            }

            navToggle.addEventListener('click', function() {
                mobileNav.classList.contains('open') ? closeMobile() : openMobile();
            });

            mobileClose.addEventListener('click', closeMobile);
            mobileOverlay.addEventListener('click', closeMobile);

            document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
                if (e.key === 'Escape' && mobileNav.classList.contains('open')) closeMobile();
            });

            const revealObserver = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
                entries.forEach(function(entry) {
                    if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                        entry.target.classList.add('in');
                        revealObserver.unobserve(entry.target);
                    }
                });
            }, { threshold: 0.08, rootMargin: '0px 0px -40px 0px' });

            document.querySelectorAll('.reveal, .reveal-stagger').forEach(function(el) {
                revealObserver.observe(el);
            });

            let chatOpen = false;
            chatToggle.addEventListener('click', function() {
                chatOpen = !chatOpen;
                chatToggle.classList.toggle('active', chatOpen);
                chatBubble.classList.toggle('open', chatOpen);
                chatToggle.querySelector('.icon').innerHTML = chatOpen ? '<i class="bi bi-x-lg"></i>' :
                    '<i class="bi bi-chat-dots-fill"></i>';
            });

            document.querySelectorAll('.chat-option').forEach(function(btn) {
                btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                    const response = this.dataset.response;
                    const msgEl = document.getElementById('chatMessage');
                    msgEl.textContent = '';
                    let i = 0;
                    const chars = response.split('');
                    const interval = setInterval(function() {
                        if (i < chars.length) {
                            msgEl.textContent += chars[i];
                            i++;
                        } else {
                            clearInterval(interval);
                        }
                    }, 12);
                });
            });

            document.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip').forEach(function(chip) {
                chip.addEventListener('click', function() {
                    document.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                    this.classList.add('active');
                });
            });

            document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                const btn = e.target.closest('.btn-gold, .btn-outline, .btn-view-more');
                if (!btn) return;
                const rect = btn.getBoundingClientRect();
                const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
                const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
                const ripple = document.createElement('span');
                ripple.className = 'ripple';
                ripple.style.cssText =
                    'position:absolute;border-radius:50%;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.25);width:20px;height:20px;left:' +
                    (x - 10) + 'px;top:' + (y - 10) + 'px;pointer-events:none;transform:scale(0);animation:rippleAnim 0.6s ease-out forwards;';
                btn.style.position = 'relative';
                btn.style.overflow = 'hidden';
                btn.appendChild(ripple);
                setTimeout(function() { ripple.remove(); }, 650);
            });

            if (!document.getElementById('rippleStyles')) {
                const style = document.createElement('style');
                style.id = 'rippleStyles';
                style.textContent = '@keyframes rippleAnim { to { transform: scale(4); opacity: 0; } }';
                document.head.appendChild(style);
            }

            console.log('✦ KnotWork — Mentors ✦');

        })();
    </script>

</body>
</html>

    <!-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════
    SCRIPTS
    ═══════════════════════════════════════════ -->
    <script>
        (function() {
            'use strict';

            const nav = document.getElementById('mainNav');
            const navToggle = document.getElementById('navToggle');
            const mobileNav = document.getElementById('mobileNav');
            const mobileOverlay = document.getElementById('mobileOverlay');
            const mobileClose = document.getElementById('mobileClose');
            const body = document.body;
            const backToTop = document.getElementById('backToTop');
            const chatToggle = document.getElementById('chatToggle');
            const chatBubble = document.getElementById('chatBubble');

            window.addEventListener('scroll', function() {
                nav.classList.toggle('scrolled', window.scrollY > 20);
                backToTop.classList.toggle('visible', window.scrollY > 400);
            }, { passive: true });

            backToTop.addEventListener('click', function() {
                window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
            });

            function openMobile() {
                mobileNav.classList.add('open');
                mobileOverlay.classList.add('open');
                navToggle.classList.add('active');
                navToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
                body.classList.add('overflow-hidden');
            }

            function closeMobile() {
                mobileNav.classList.remove('open');
                mobileOverlay.classList.remove('open');
                navToggle.classList.remove('active');
                navToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                body.classList.remove('overflow-hidden');
            }

            navToggle.addEventListener('click', function() {
                mobileNav.classList.contains('open') ? closeMobile() : openMobile();
            });

            mobileClose.addEventListener('click', closeMobile);
            mobileOverlay.addEventListener('click', closeMobile);

            document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
                if (e.key === 'Escape' && mobileNav.classList.contains('open')) closeMobile();
            });

            const revealObserver = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
                entries.forEach(function(entry) {
                    if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                        entry.target.classList.add('in');
                        revealObserver.unobserve(entry.target);
                    }
                });
            }, { threshold: 0.08, rootMargin: '0px 0px -40px 0px' });

            document.querySelectorAll('.reveal, .reveal-stagger').forEach(function(el) {
                revealObserver.observe(el);
            });

            let chatOpen = false;
            chatToggle.addEventListener('click', function() {
                chatOpen = !chatOpen;
                chatToggle.classList.toggle('active', chatOpen);
                chatBubble.classList.toggle('open', chatOpen);
                chatToggle.querySelector('.icon').innerHTML = chatOpen ? '<i class="bi bi-x-lg"></i>' :
                    '<i class="bi bi-chat-dots-fill"></i>';
            });

            document.querySelectorAll('.chat-option').forEach(function(btn) {
                btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                    const response = this.dataset.response;
                    const msgEl = document.getElementById('chatMessage');
                    msgEl.textContent = '';
                    let i = 0;
                    const chars = response.split('');
                    const interval = setInterval(function() {
                        if (i < chars.length) {
                            msgEl.textContent += chars[i];
                            i++;
                        } else {
                            clearInterval(interval);
                        }
                    }, 12);
                });
            });

            document.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip').forEach(function(chip) {
                chip.addEventListener('click', function() {
                    document.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                    this.classList.add('active');
                });
            });

            document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                const btn = e.target.closest('.btn-gold, .btn-outline, .btn-view-more');
                if (!btn) return;
                const rect = btn.getBoundingClientRect();
                const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
                const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
                const ripple = document.createElement('span');
                ripple.className = 'ripple';
                ripple.style.cssText =
                    'position:absolute;border-radius:50%;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.25);width:20px;height:20px;left:' +
                    (x - 10) + 'px;top:' + (y - 10) + 'px;pointer-events:none;transform:scale(0);animation:rippleAnim 0.6s ease-out forwards;';
                btn.style.position = 'relative';
                btn.style.overflow = 'hidden';
                btn.appendChild(ripple);
                setTimeout(function() { ripple.remove(); }, 650);
            });

            if (!document.getElementById('rippleStyles')) {
                const style = document.createElement('style');
                style.id = 'rippleStyles';
                style.textContent = '@keyframes rippleAnim { to { transform: scale(4); opacity: 0; } }';
                document.head.appendChild(style);
            }

            console.log('✦ KnotWork — Communities ✦');

        })();
    </script>

</body>
</html>

    <!-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════
    SCRIPTS
    ═══════════════════════════════════════════ -->
    <script>
        (function() {
            'use strict';

            const nav = document.getElementById('mainNav');
            const navToggle = document.getElementById('navToggle');
            const mobileNav = document.getElementById('mobileNav');
            const mobileOverlay = document.getElementById('mobileOverlay');
            const mobileClose = document.getElementById('mobileClose');
            const body = document.body;
            const backToTop = document.getElementById('backToTop');
            const chatToggle = document.getElementById('chatToggle');
            const chatBubble = document.getElementById('chatBubble');

            window.addEventListener('scroll', function() {
                nav.classList.toggle('scrolled', window.scrollY > 20);
                backToTop.classList.toggle('visible', window.scrollY > 400);
            }, { passive: true });

            backToTop.addEventListener('click', function() {
                window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
            });

            function openMobile() {
                mobileNav.classList.add('open');
                mobileOverlay.classList.add('open');
                navToggle.classList.add('active');
                navToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
                body.classList.add('overflow-hidden');
            }

            function closeMobile() {
                mobileNav.classList.remove('open');
                mobileOverlay.classList.remove('open');
                navToggle.classList.remove('active');
                navToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                body.classList.remove('overflow-hidden');
            }

            navToggle.addEventListener('click', function() {
                mobileNav.classList.contains('open') ? closeMobile() : openMobile();
            });

            mobileClose.addEventListener('click', closeMobile);
            mobileOverlay.addEventListener('click', closeMobile);

            document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
                if (e.key === 'Escape' && mobileNav.classList.contains('open')) closeMobile();
            });

            const revealObserver = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
                entries.forEach(function(entry) {
                    if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                        entry.target.classList.add('in');
                        revealObserver.unobserve(entry.target);
                    }
                });
            }, { threshold: 0.08, rootMargin: '0px 0px -40px 0px' });

            document.querySelectorAll('.reveal, .reveal-stagger').forEach(function(el) {
                revealObserver.observe(el);
            });

            let chatOpen = false;
            chatToggle.addEventListener('click', function() {
                chatOpen = !chatOpen;
                chatToggle.classList.toggle('active', chatOpen);
                chatBubble.classList.toggle('open', chatOpen);
                chatToggle.querySelector('.icon').innerHTML = chatOpen ? '<i class="bi bi-x-lg"></i>' :
                    '<i class="bi bi-chat-dots-fill"></i>';
            });

            document.querySelectorAll('.chat-option').forEach(function(btn) {
                btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                    const response = this.dataset.response;
                    const msgEl = document.getElementById('chatMessage');
                    msgEl.textContent = '';
                    let i = 0;
                    const chars = response.split('');
                    const interval = setInterval(function() {
                        if (i < chars.length) {
                            msgEl.textContent += chars[i];
                            i++;
                        } else {
                            clearInterval(interval);
                        }
                    }, 12);
                });
            });

            // ── FAQ accordion ──
            document.querySelectorAll('.faq-item .faq-question').forEach(function(q) {
                q.addEventListener('click', function() {
                    const item = this.parentElement;
                    const wasOpen = item.classList.contains('open');
                    // Close all others
                    document.querySelectorAll('.faq-item').forEach(function(el) {
                        el.classList.remove('open');
                    });
                    if (!wasOpen) {
                        item.classList.add('open');
                    }
                });
            });

            document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                const btn = e.target.closest('.btn-gold, .btn-outline, .btn-jade, .btn-view-more');
                if (!btn) return;
                const rect = btn.getBoundingClientRect();
                const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
                const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
                const ripple = document.createElement('span');
                ripple.className = 'ripple';
                ripple.style.cssText =
                    'position:absolute;border-radius:50%;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.25);width:20px;height:20px;left:' +
                    (x - 10) + 'px;top:' + (y - 10) + 'px;pointer-events:none;transform:scale(0);animation:rippleAnim 0.6s ease-out forwards;';
                btn.style.position = 'relative';
                btn.style.overflow = 'hidden';
                btn.appendChild(ripple);
                setTimeout(function() { ripple.remove(); }, 650);
            });

            if (!document.getElementById('rippleStyles')) {
                const style = document.createElement('style');
                style.id = 'rippleStyles';
                style.textContent = '@keyframes rippleAnim { to { transform: scale(4); opacity: 0; } }';
                document.head.appendChild(style);
            }

            console.log('✦ KnotWork — Pricing ✦');

        })();
    </script>

</body>
</html>

    <!-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════
    SCRIPTS
    ═══════════════════════════════════════════ -->
    <script>
        (function() {
            'use strict';

            const nav = document.getElementById('mainNav');
            const navToggle = document.getElementById('navToggle');
            const mobileNav = document.getElementById('mobileNav');
            const mobileOverlay = document.getElementById('mobileOverlay');
            const mobileClose = document.getElementById('mobileClose');
            const body = document.body;
            const backToTop = document.getElementById('backToTop');
            const chatToggle = document.getElementById('chatToggle');
            const chatBubble = document.getElementById('chatBubble');

            window.addEventListener('scroll', function() {
                nav.classList.toggle('scrolled', window.scrollY > 20);
                backToTop.classList.toggle('visible', window.scrollY > 400);
            }, { passive: true });

            backToTop.addEventListener('click', function() {
                window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
            });

            function openMobile() {
                mobileNav.classList.add('open');
                mobileOverlay.classList.add('open');
                navToggle.classList.add('active');
                navToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
                body.classList.add('overflow-hidden');
            }

            function closeMobile() {
                mobileNav.classList.remove('open');
                mobileOverlay.classList.remove('open');
                navToggle.classList.remove('active');
                navToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                body.classList.remove('overflow-hidden');
            }

            navToggle.addEventListener('click', function() {
                mobileNav.classList.contains('open') ? closeMobile() : openMobile();
            });

            mobileClose.addEventListener('click', closeMobile);
            mobileOverlay.addEventListener('click', closeMobile);

            document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
                if (e.key === 'Escape' && mobileNav.classList.contains('open')) closeMobile();
            });

            const revealObserver = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
                entries.forEach(function(entry) {
                    if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                        entry.target.classList.add('in');
                        revealObserver.unobserve(entry.target);
                    }
                });
            }, { threshold: 0.08, rootMargin: '0px 0px -40px 0px' });

            document.querySelectorAll('.reveal, .reveal-stagger').forEach(function(el) {
                revealObserver.observe(el);
            });

            let chatOpen = false;
            chatToggle.addEventListener('click', function() {
                chatOpen = !chatOpen;
                chatToggle.classList.toggle('active', chatOpen);
                chatBubble.classList.toggle('open', chatOpen);
                chatToggle.querySelector('.icon').innerHTML = chatOpen ? '<i class="bi bi-x-lg"></i>' :
                    '<i class="bi bi-chat-dots-fill"></i>';
            });

            document.querySelectorAll('.chat-option').forEach(function(btn) {
                btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                    const response = this.dataset.response;
                    const msgEl = document.getElementById('chatMessage');
                    msgEl.textContent = '';
                    let i = 0;
                    const chars = response.split('');
                    const interval = setInterval(function() {
                        if (i < chars.length) {
                            msgEl.textContent += chars[i];
                            i++;
                        } else {
                            clearInterval(interval);
                        }
                    }, 12);
                });
            });

            document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                const btn = e.target.closest('.btn-gold, .btn-outline, .btn-view-more');
                if (!btn) return;
                const rect = btn.getBoundingClientRect();
                const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
                const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
                const ripple = document.createElement('span');
                ripple.className = 'ripple';
                ripple.style.cssText =
                    'position:absolute;border-radius:50%;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.25);width:20px;height:20px;left:' +
                    (x - 10) + 'px;top:' + (y - 10) + 'px;pointer-events:none;transform:scale(0);animation:rippleAnim 0.6s ease-out forwards;';
                btn.style.position = 'relative';
                btn.style.overflow = 'hidden';
                btn.appendChild(ripple);
                setTimeout(function() { ripple.remove(); }, 650);
            });

            if (!document.getElementById('rippleStyles')) {
                const style = document.createElement('style');
                style.id = 'rippleStyles';
                style.textContent = '@keyframes rippleAnim { to { transform: scale(4); opacity: 0; } }';
                document.head.appendChild(style);
            }

            console.log('✦ KnotWork — About ✦');

        })();
    </script>

</body>
</html>

                <span class="text-[#8A8A9A] text-[0.8rem]">Made for people who'd rather trade than pay.</span>
            </div>
        </div>
    </footer>

    <!-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════
    SCRIPTS
    ═══════════════════════════════════════════ -->
    <script>
        (function() {
            'use strict';

            const nav = document.getElementById('mainNav');
            const navToggle = document.getElementById('navToggle');
            const mobileNav = document.getElementById('mobileNav');
            const mobileOverlay = document.getElementById('mobileOverlay');
            const mobileClose = document.getElementById('mobileClose');
            const body = document.body;
            const backToTop = document.getElementById('backToTop');
            const chatToggle = document.getElementById('chatToggle');
            const chatBubble = document.getElementById('chatBubble');

            window.addEventListener('scroll', function() {
                nav.classList.toggle('scrolled', window.scrollY > 20);
                backToTop.classList.toggle('visible', window.scrollY > 400);
            }, { passive: true });

            backToTop.addEventListener('click', function() {
                window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
            });

            function openMobile() {
                mobileNav.classList.add('open');
                mobileOverlay.classList.add('open');
                navToggle.classList.add('active');
                navToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
                body.classList.add('overflow-hidden');
            }

            function closeMobile() {
                mobileNav.classList.remove('open');
                mobileOverlay.classList.remove('open');
                navToggle.classList.remove('active');
                navToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                body.classList.remove('overflow-hidden');
            }

            navToggle.addEventListener('click', function() {
                mobileNav.classList.contains('open') ? closeMobile() : openMobile();
            });

            mobileClose.addEventListener('click', closeMobile);
            mobileOverlay.addEventListener('click', closeMobile);

            document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
                if (e.key === 'Escape' && mobileNav.classList.contains('open')) closeMobile();
            });

            const revealObserver = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
                entries.forEach(function(entry) {
                    if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                        entry.target.classList.add('in');
                        revealObserver.unobserve(entry.target);
                    }
                });
            }, { threshold: 0.08, rootMargin: '0px 0px -40px 0px' });

            document.querySelectorAll('.reveal, .reveal-stagger').forEach(function(el) {
                revealObserver.observe(el);
            });

            let chatOpen = false;
            chatToggle.addEventListener('click', function() {
                chatOpen = !chatOpen;
                chatToggle.classList.toggle('active', chatOpen);
                chatBubble.classList.toggle('open', chatOpen);
                chatToggle.querySelector('.icon').innerHTML = chatOpen ? '<i class="bi bi-x-lg"></i>' :
                    '<i class="bi bi-chat-dots-fill"></i>';
            });

            document.querySelectorAll('.chat-option').forEach(function(btn) {
                btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                    const response = this.dataset.response;
                    const msgEl = document.getElementById('chatMessage');
                    msgEl.textContent = '';
                    let i = 0;
                    const chars = response.split('');
                    const interval = setInterval(function() {
                        if (i < chars.length) {
                            msgEl.textContent += chars[i];
                            i++;
                        } else {
                            clearInterval(interval);
                        }
                    }, 12);
                });
            });

            document.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip').forEach(function(chip) {
                chip.addEventListener('click', function() {
                    document.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                    this.classList.add('active');
                });
            });

            document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                const btn = e.target.closest('.btn-gold, .btn-outline, .btn-view-more');
                if (!btn) return;
                const rect = btn.getBoundingClientRect();
                const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
                const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
                const ripple = document.createElement('span');
                ripple.className = 'ripple';
                ripple.style.cssText =
                    'position:absolute;border-radius:50%;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.25);width:20px;height:20px;left:' +
                    (x - 10) + 'px;top:' + (y - 10) + 'px;pointer-events:none;transform:scale(0);animation:rippleAnim 0.6s ease-out forwards;';
                btn.style.position = 'relative';
                btn.style.overflow = 'hidden';
                btn.appendChild(ripple);
                setTimeout(function() { ripple.remove(); }, 650);
            });

            if (!document.getElementById('rippleStyles')) {
                const style = document.createElement('style');
                style.id = 'rippleStyles';
                style.textContent = '@keyframes rippleAnim { to { transform: scale(4); opacity: 0; } }';
                document.head.appendChild(style);
            }

            console.log('✦ KnotWork — Blog ✦');

        })();
    </script>

</body>
</html>

    FAQ
    ═══════════════════════════════════════════ -->
    <section class="py-16 bg-[#F8F6F1] border-y border-[rgba(26,26,46,0.06)] z-[1] relative">
        <div class="max-w-[820px] mx-auto px-6">
            <div class="text-center mb-10 reveal">
                <span class="font-mono text-[0.7rem] uppercase tracking-[0.14em] font-semibold text-[#D4A853] block mb-2">FAQ</span>
                <h2 class="font-display text-[clamp(1.6rem,2.5vw,2.2rem)] font-bold tracking-[-0.02em] text-[#1A1A2E]">Frequently asked questions</h2>
            </div>
            <div class="bg-white border border-[rgba(26,26,46,0.06)] rounded-[18px] p-6 reveal">
                <div class="faq-item">
                    <div class="faq-question">
                        <span>How do I get started with KnotWork?</span>
                        <span class="faq-icon"><i class="bi bi-chevron-down"></i></span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="faq-answer">Create your profile, list the skills you can teach and the skills you want to learn. Our AI will start matching you right away. You'll receive notifications when a good match is found.</div>
                </div>
                <div class="faq-item">
                    <div class="faq-question">
                        <span>How do the video calls work?</span>
                        <span class="faq-icon"><i class="bi bi-chevron-down"></i></span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="faq-answer">All sessions happen in our built‑in video room. No downloads, no extra software — just click and start teaching or learning. You can share your screen and use a collaborative whiteboard.</div>
                </div>
                <div class="faq-item">
                    <div class="faq-question">
                        <span>Is KnotWork really free?</span>
                        <span class="faq-icon"><i class="bi bi-chevron-down"></i></span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="faq-answer">Yes! KnotWork is built on exchange, not money. You teach one hour, you learn one hour. That's the only currency. No hidden fees, no surprises.</div>
                </div>
                <div class="faq-item">
                    <div class="faq-question">
                        <span>Can I teach multiple skills?</span>
                        <span class="faq-icon"><i class="bi bi-chevron-down"></i></span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="faq-answer">Absolutely. List as many skills as you'd like — you'll be matched with learners who need each one. You can also set your availability for each skill separately.</div>
                </div>
                <div class="faq-item">
                    <div class="faq-question">
                        <span>How are ratings used?</span>
                        <span class="faq-icon"><i class="bi bi-chevron-down"></i></span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="faq-answer">After each session, both sides rate the experience. Honest ratings help the community trust each other and improve. Ratings are visible on your profile.</div>
                </div>
                <div class="faq-item">
                    <div class="faq-question">
                        <span>Is KnotWork global?</span>
                        <span class="faq-icon"><i class="bi bi-chevron-down"></i></span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="faq-answer">Yes — we have members in 116 countries. You can learn from someone across the world, or down the street. Time zones are automatically handled in scheduling.</div>
                </div>
                <div class="faq-item">
                    <div class="faq-question">
                        <span>What if I need to cancel a session?</span>
                        <span class="faq-icon"><i class="bi bi-chevron-down"></i></span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="faq-answer">You can cancel from your calendar up to 2 hours before the session. We encourage you to reschedule rather than cancel to keep the exchange fair.</div>
                </div>
                <div class="faq-item">
                    <div class="faq-question">
                        <span>How do I report an issue or a user?</span>
                        <span class="faq-icon"><i class="bi bi-chevron-down"></i></span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="faq-answer">You can report any issue from the session page or from a user's profile. Our moderation team reviews all reports within 24 hours.</div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

    <!-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════
    SCRIPTS
    ═══════════════════════════════════════════ -->
    <script>
        (function() {
            'use strict';

            const nav = document.getElementById('mainNav');
            const navToggle = document.getElementById('navToggle');
            const mobileNav = document.getElementById('mobileNav');
            const mobileOverlay = document.getElementById('mobileOverlay');
            const mobileClose = document.getElementById('mobileClose');
            const body = document.body;
            const backToTop = document.getElementById('backToTop');
            const chatToggle = document.getElementById('chatToggle');
            const chatBubble = document.getElementById('chatBubble');

            window.addEventListener('scroll', function() {
                nav.classList.toggle('scrolled', window.scrollY > 20);
                backToTop.classList.toggle('visible', window.scrollY > 400);
            }, { passive: true });

            backToTop.addEventListener('click', function() {
                window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
            });

            function openMobile() {
                mobileNav.classList.add('open');
                mobileOverlay.classList.add('open');
                navToggle.classList.add('active');
                navToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
                body.classList.add('overflow-hidden');
            }

            function closeMobile() {
                mobileNav.classList.remove('open');
                mobileOverlay.classList.remove('open');
                navToggle.classList.remove('active');
                navToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                body.classList.remove('overflow-hidden');
            }

            navToggle.addEventListener('click', function() {
                mobileNav.classList.contains('open') ? closeMobile() : openMobile();
            });

            mobileClose.addEventListener('click', closeMobile);
            mobileOverlay.addEventListener('click', closeMobile);

            document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
                if (e.key === 'Escape' && mobileNav.classList.contains('open')) closeMobile();
            });

            const revealObserver = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
                entries.forEach(function(entry) {
                    if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                        entry.target.classList.add('in');
                        revealObserver.unobserve(entry.target);
                    }
                });
            }, { threshold: 0.08, rootMargin: '0px 0px -40px 0px' });

            document.querySelectorAll('.reveal, .reveal-stagger').forEach(function(el) {
                revealObserver.observe(el);
            });

            let chatOpen = false;
            chatToggle.addEventListener('click', function() {
                chatOpen = !chatOpen;
                chatToggle.classList.toggle('active', chatOpen);
                chatBubble.classList.toggle('open', chatOpen);
                chatToggle.querySelector('.icon').innerHTML = chatOpen ? '<i class="bi bi-x-lg"></i>' :
                    '<i class="bi bi-chat-dots-fill"></i>';
            });

            document.querySelectorAll('.chat-option').forEach(function(btn) {
                btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                    const response = this.dataset.response;
                    const msgEl = document.getElementById('chatMessage');
                    msgEl.textContent = '';
                    let i = 0;
                    const chars = response.split('');
                    const interval = setInterval(function() {
                        if (i < chars.length) {
                            msgEl.textContent += chars[i];
                            i++;
                        } else {
                            clearInterval(interval);
                        }
                    }, 12);
                });
            });

            // ── FAQ accordion ──
            document.querySelectorAll('.faq-item .faq-question').forEach(function(q) {
                q.addEventListener('click', function() {
                    const item = this.parentElement;
                    const wasOpen = item.classList.contains('open');
                    document.querySelectorAll('.faq-item').forEach(function(el) {
                        el.classList.remove('open');
                    });
                    if (!wasOpen) {
                        item.classList.add('open');
                    }
                });
            });

            document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                const btn = e.target.closest('.btn-gold, .btn-outline, .btn-view-more');
                if (!btn) return;
                const rect = btn.getBoundingClientRect();
                const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
                const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
                const ripple = document.createElement('span');
                ripple.className = 'ripple';
                ripple.style.cssText =
                    'position:absolute;border-radius:50%;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.25);width:20px;height:20px;left:' +
                    (x - 10) + 'px;top:' + (y - 10) + 'px;pointer-events:none;transform:scale(0);animation:rippleAnim 0.6s ease-out forwards;';
                btn.style.position = 'relative';
                btn.style.overflow = 'hidden';
                btn.appendChild(ripple);
                setTimeout(function() { ripple.remove(); }, 650);
            });

            if (!document.getElementById('rippleStyles')) {
                const style = document.createElement('style');
                style.id = 'rippleStyles';
                style.textContent = '@keyframes rippleAnim { to { transform: scale(4); opacity: 0; } }';
                document.head.appendChild(style);
            }

            console.log('✦ KnotWork — Help ✦');

        })();
    </script>

</body>
</html>
